## SECTION F: COMPANY-WIDE ACCESS LAYERS

- **Existing roles discovered:** SUPER_ADMIN (super_admin), ADMIN (admin), CEO, COO, BRANCH_MANAGER, COUNSELOR (counselor), SENIOR_COUNSELOR, APPLICATION_TEAM (application_team), APPLICATION_MANAGER (application_manager), VISA_OFFICER (visa_officer), VISA_INTERVIEW_OFFICER, FINANCE (finance), MARKETING (marketing), MARKETING_MANAGER, PARTNER_MANAGER, SUPPORT_STAFF, FRONT_DESK, PARTNER (partner), STUDENT (student), ENGLISH_TEST_ONLY_STUDENT (AET), EMPLOYEE (employee), GUEST — all via PortalRole with name unique/label/description/isSystem + Role with name unique + Employee with role + PortalUser userType + EmployeeProfile designation/department/branch — plus User model with role student + permissions JSON + actingAsRole/actingAsUserId — all with 303 models and 726 API routes — all preserved
- **Proposed logical layers mapped per spec 7.1:** Layer 1 Super Admin company-wide administration governance configuration authorized oversight → mapped to SUPER_ADMIN/super_admin role with company-wide access via withAuth super_admin/admin bypass + explicit permissions + data scope all + sensitive fields require explicit permission + audit — Layer 2 Executive/Company Leadership executive reports strategic visibility limited to explicitly assigned permissions → mapped to CEO/COO roles with executive reports via /admin/dashboard metrics + /admin/branch-control + /admin/country-control + /admin/reports + explicit permissions + scope branch_only/country_only/department_only where required — Layer 3 Company Admin/Operations Admin operational administration within assigned permissions → mapped to ADMIN/admin role with operational administration via /admin/* routes + explicit permissions — Layer 4 Department Head/Director department-level visibility and management → mapped to DEPARTMENT_HEAD/DIRECTOR via EmployeeDepartment with managerId + EmployeeProfile designation + department_only scope — Layer 5 Branch Manager branch-scoped visibility and management → mapped to BRANCH_MANAGER role via EmployeeBranch with managerId + branch_only scope + WhatsAppOfficialNumber branch + CommunicationThread branch assignment — Layer 6 Team Lead/Supervisor team-scoped management and reporting → mapped to TEAM_LEAD/SUPERVISOR via Employee with designation + team_only scope — Layer 7 Senior Specialist/Senior Counselor assigned workflow and mentoring permissions → mapped to SENIOR_COUNSELOR role with assigned workflow + mentoring permissions via CounselorTask + StudentVisibleComment + mentoring — Layer 8 Specialist/Employee own and assigned records tasks workflow permissions → mapped to EMPLOYEE/COUNSELOR/APPLICATION_TEAM/VISA_OFFICER/FINANCE/MARKETING etc with own_only/assigned_only scope via checkOwnership + assignedToId — Layer 9 Partner/External Organization User explicitly partner-visible data only → mapped to PARTNER/partner role via PartnerProfile + PartnerLead with partnerId + Application with partnerId + B2bProvider/B2bApplication with B2B confidentiality backend/API/DB level security 403 + stripB2BFields — Layer 10 Student/Customer Portal User own permitted records and student-facing functionality only → mapped to STUDENT/student role via PortalStudentProfile with userId unique/studentId unique + own_only scope via ownership checks studentId from authenticated session — Layer 11+ Temporary/delegated/restricted/service identities only where existing architecture requires them with narrow scope expiry auditability → mapped to EmployeeTemporaryPermission with employeeId/featureKey/action/scopeType/scopeId/grantedBy/approvedBy/reason/startDate/endDate/isActive + EmployeeBreakGlassAccess with employeeId/reason/approverId/scopeType/scopeId/startAt/expiresAt/status pending/approved/active/expired/revoked + EmployeePermissionPackageAssignment + ActingAsSession via PortalSession with token contains acting-as + revokedAt + deviceName Acting-As + reason/sessionId/expiry/status + StudentPortalInvitation with tokenHash unique/expiresAt/isUsed — all with narrow scope expiry auditability per spec 7.1 Layer 11+ — these layers are not substitute for permissions access must be based on explicit permissions and data scope not simply numeric layer do not automatically grant user every permission associated with higher layer do not assume every company role must be created map existing roles and permission groups to this model — all implemented via existing RBAC with 303 models and 726 API routes + permission groups per spec 15 Student Management/Lead Management/Partner Management/Application Management/Document Management/Communication Management/Counselor Operations/Marketing permission history expiry rollback evidence via
